Idempotent: regenerates today's rollup\n# only when raw log mtime > rollup mtime.\n#\n# Test strategy: stage temp PROJECT_DIR with various .drift-signals.log\n# fixtures; invoke hook; assert rollup file existence + content sections.\n#\n# 6 test cases:\n#   TC1 — raw log absent → \"raw log absent; skip\" + no rollup file\n#   TC2 — raw log exists, no rollup yet → rollup written with summary\n#   TC3 — rollup current (raw mtime <= rollup mtime) → \"rollup current; skip\"\n#   TC4 — multiple severity levels → HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW counts in summary\n#   TC5 — file= references → \"Files referenced\" section populated\n#   TC6 — HIGH entries → \"Last 10 HIGH-severity entries\" code block populated\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../drift-rollup-daily.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nRAW_LOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.drift-signals.log\"\nROLLUP_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/drift-logs\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nTODAY=\"$(date +%Y-%m-%d)\"\nROLLUP_FILE=\"$ROLLUP_DIR/$TODAY-rollup.md\"\n\nrun_hook() {\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: raw log absent → skip ---\nclean_state\nrun_hook\nif [ -f \"$ROLLUP_FILE\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: rollup should NOT be written when raw log absent\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"raw log absent\" \"$HOOK_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected 'raw log absent' message in hook log\"\n  cat \"$HOOK_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || echo \"(no hook log)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: raw log absent → no rollup + skip message\"\n\n# --- TC2: raw log exists, no rollup yet → write rollup ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$RAW_LOG\" <<EOF\n[${TODAY}T10:00:00+00:00] D1-LOC-CEILING severity=MEDIUM file=foo.md lines=180 ceiling=150\n[${TODAY}T11:00:00+00:00] D5-MISSING-CITATION severity=HIGH file=bar.md\nEOF\nrun_hook\nif [ ! -f \"$ROLLUP_FILE\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: rollup should be written\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"# Drift Rollup\" \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected '# Drift Rollup' header\"\n  cat \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"Total signals today.*: 2\" \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected total=2\"\n  cat \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: raw log → rollup written with summary\"\n\n# --- TC3: rollup current (raw mtime <= rollup) → skip ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$RAW_LOG\" <<EOF\n[${TODAY}T10:00:00+00:00] D1-LOC-CEILING severity=MEDIUM file=foo.md\nEOF\nmkdir -p \"$ROLLUP_DIR\"\necho \"# stale rollup placeholder\" > \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"\n# Make rollup newer than raw log\ntouch \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"\nsleep 1\n# Make raw log OLDER than rollup\ntouch -t 200001010000.00 \"$RAW_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || touch -d \"2000-01-01\" \"$RAW_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true\nrun_hook\nif ! grep -q \"rollup current\" \"$HOOK_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected 'rollup current; skip' message\"\n  cat \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n# Rollup file content should be UNCHANGED (still placeholder)\nif ! grep -q \"stale rollup placeholder\" \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: rollup should NOT have been regenerated\"\n  cat \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: rollup current (raw mtime ≤ rollup) → skip\"\n\n# --- TC4: multiple severity levels → counts in summary ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$RAW_LOG\" <<EOF\n[${TODAY}T10:00:00+00:00] D1-LOC-CEILING severity=HIGH file=a.md\n[${TODAY}T10:01:00+00:00] D1-LOC-CEILING severity=HIGH file=b.md\n[${TODAY}T10:02:00+00:00] D2-SELF-ATTEST severity=MEDIUM file=c.md\n[${TODAY}T10:03:00+00:00] D4-SPEC-DANGLING-REF severity=LOW file=d.md\nEOF\nrun_hook\nif ! grep -q \"HIGH.*: 2\" \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected HIGH count=2\"\n  cat \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"MEDIUM.*: 1\" \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected MEDIUM count=1\"\n  cat \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"LOW.*: 1\" \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected LOW count=1\"\n  cat \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: severity counts populated correctly\"\n\n# --- TC5: file= references → \"Files referenced\" section ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$RAW_LOG\" <<EOF\n[${TODAY}T10:00:00+00:00] D1-LOC-CEILING severity=HIGH file=alpha.md lines=300\n[${TODAY}T10:01:00+00:00] D5-MISSING-CITATION severity=HIGH file=beta.md\n[${TODAY}T10:02:00+00:00] D7-NO-BEAR-CASE severity=MEDIUM file=alpha.md\nEOF\nrun_hook\nif ! Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../drift-rollup-daily.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nRAW_LOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.drift-signals.log\"\nROLLUP_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/drift-logs\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nTODAY=\"$(date +%Y-%m-%d)\"\nROLLUP_FILE=\"$ROLLUP_DIR/$TODAY-rollup.md\"\n\nrun_hook() {\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: raw log absent → skip ---\nclean_state\nrun_hook\nif [ -f \"$ROLLUP_FILE\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: rollup should NOT be written when raw log absent\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"raw log absent\" \"$HOOK_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected 'raw log absent' message in hook log\"\n  cat \"$HOOK_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || echo \"(no hook log)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: raw log absent → no rollup + skip message\"\n\n# --- TC2: raw log exists, no rollup yet → write rollup ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$RAW_LOG\" <<EOF\n[${TODAY}T10:00:00+00:00] D1-LOC-CEILING severity=MEDIUM file=foo.md lines=180 ceiling=150\n[${TODAY}T11:00:00+00:00] D5-MISSING-CITATION severity=HIGH file=bar.md\nEOF\nrun_hook\nif [ ! -f \"$ROLLUP_FILE\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: rollup should be written\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"# Drift Rollup\" \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected '# Drift Rollup' header\"\n  cat \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"Total signals today.*: 2\" \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected total=2\"\n  cat \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: raw log → rollup written with summary\"\n\n# --- TC3: rollup current (raw mtime <= rollup) → skip ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$RAW_LOG\" <<EOF\n[${TODAY}T10:00:00+00:00] D1-LOC-CEILING severity=MEDIUM file=foo.md\nEOF\nmkdir -p \"$ROLLUP_DIR\"\necho \"# stale rollup placeholder\" > \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"\n# Make rollup newer than raw log\ntouch \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"\nsleep 1\n# Make raw log OLDER than rollup\ntouch -t 200001010000.00 \"$RAW_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || touch -d \"2000-01-01\" \"$RAW_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true\nrun_hook\nif ! grep -q \"rollup current\" \"$HOOK_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected 'rollup current; skip' message\"\n  cat \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n# Rollup file content should be UNCHANGED (still placeholder)\nif ! grep -q \"stale rollup placeholder\" \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: rollup should NOT have been regenerated\"\n  cat \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: rollup current (raw mtime ≤ rollup) → skip\"\n\n# --- TC4: multiple severity levels → counts in summary ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$RAW_LOG\" <<EOF\n[${TODAY}T10:00:00+00:00] D1-LOC-CEILING severity=HIGH file=a.md\n[${TODAY}T10:01:00+00:00] D1-LOC-CEILING severity=HIGH file=b.md\n[${TODAY}T10:02:00+00:00] D2-SELF-ATTEST severity=MEDIUM file=c.md\n[${TODAY}T10:03:00+00:00] D4-SPEC-DANGLING-REF severity=LOW file=d.md\nEOF\nrun_hook\nif ! grep -q \"HIGH.*: 2\" \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected HIGH count=2\"\n  cat \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"MEDIUM.*: 1\" \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected MEDIUM count=1\"\n  cat \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q '\"rate\":0' \"$JSON_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected rate=0\"\n  cat \"$JSON_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: no failure_mode in events → rate=0\"\n\n# --- TC3: events with failure_mode → with_fm>0 + dist ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$EVENTS_DIR\"\ncat > \"$EVENTS_DIR/2026-05-05.ndjson\" <<'EOF'\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"foo\",\"failure_mode\":\"timeout\"}}\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:01:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"bar\"}}\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:02:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"baz\",\"failure_mode\":\"validation\"}}\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:03:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"qux\",\"failure_mode\":\"timeout\"}}\nEOF\nrun_hook --quiet >/dev/null\nJSON_FILE=$(latest_metric_file)\nif ! grep -q '\"timeout\":2' \"$JSON_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected dist timeout=2\"\n  cat \"$JSON_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q '\"validation\":1' \"$JSON_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected dist validation=1\"\n  cat \"$JSON_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: events with failure_mode → with_fm=3, dist populated\"\n\n# --- TC4: --window 2 → only last 2 events counted ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$EVENTS_DIR\"\ncat > \"$EVENTS_DIR/2026-05-05.ndjson\" <<'EOF'\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"e1\",\"failure_mode\":\"timeout\"}}\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:01:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"e2\",\"failure_mode\":\"timeout\"}}\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:02:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"e3\"}}\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:03:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"e4\",\"failure_mode\":\"validation\"}}\nEOF\nrun_hook --quiet --window 2 >/dev/null\nJSON_FILE=$(latest_metric_file)\nif ! grep -q '\"timeout\":2' \"$JSON_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected dist timeout=2\"\n  cat \"$JSON_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q '\"validation\":1' \"$JSON_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected dist validation=1\"\n  cat \"$JSON_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: events with failure_mode → with_fm=3, dist populated\"\n\n# --- TC4: --window 2 → only last 2 events counted ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$EVENTS_DIR\"\ncat > \"$EVENTS_DIR/2026-05-05.ndjson\" <<'EOF'\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"e1\",\"failure_mode\":\"timeout\"}}\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:01:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"e2\",\"failure_mode\":\"timeout\"}}\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:02:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"e3\"}}\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:03:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"e4\",\"failure_mode\":\"validation\"}}\nEOF\nrun_hook --quiet --window 2 >/dev/null\nJSON_FILE=$(latest_metric_file)\nif ! Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../sync-tracker-auto-update.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: marker should NOT be created when stub missing\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: sync-tracker-update.sh missing → silent no-op\"\n\n# --- TC2: marker already exists → exit 0 idempotent (no fire) ---\nclean_state\nstage_stub\ntouch \"$(marker_path tc2)\"\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\"\necho \"# ADR\" > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions/D-099-test.md\"\nrun_hook \"tc2\"\nif [ -s \"$STUB_CALLS\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: stub should NOT be called when marker exists\"\n  cat \"$STUB_CALLS\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: marker exists → idempotent no-op\"\n\n# --- TC3: new ADR mtime <6h → SCOPE charter_match fired ---\nclean_state\nstage_stub\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\"\necho \"# ADR D-099 test\" > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions/D-099-test.md\"\nrun_hook \"tc3\"\nif [ ! -s \"$STUB_CALLS\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: stub should be called for new ADR\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"SCOPE charter_match\" \"$STUB_CALLS\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected 'SCOPE charter_match' invocation\"\n  cat \"$STUB_CALLS\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: new ADR <6h → SCOPE charter_match fired\"\n\n# --- TC4: HIGH drift today → DECISION_ROUTING drift_signal fired ---\nclean_state\nstage_stub\nTODAY=\"$(date -u +%Y-%m-%d)\"\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.drift-signals.log\" <<EOF\n[${TODAY}T10:00:00+00:00] D1-LOC-CEILING severity=HIGH file=foo.md\n[${TODAY}T10:01:00+00:00] D5-MISSING-CITATION severity=HIGH file=bar.md\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc4\"\nif ! grep -q \"DECISION_ROUTING drift_signal\" \"$STUB_CALLS\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected 'DECISION_ROUTING drift_signal' invocation\"\n  cat \"$STUB_CALLS\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: HIGH drift today → DECISION_ROUTING drift_signal fired\"\n\n# --- TC5: Q&A in answered/ <6h → DECISION_ROUTING q_and_a_resolution ---\nclean_state\nstage_stub\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\"\necho \"# answered bundle\" > \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/2026-05-05-bundle.md\"\nrun_hook \"tc5\"\nif ! grep -q \"DECISION_ROUTING q_and_a_resolution\" \"$STUB_CALLS\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected 'DECISION_ROUTING q_and_a_resolution' invocation\"\n  cat \"$STUB_CALLS\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC5: Q&A answered <6h → q_and_a_resolution fired\"\n\n# --- TC6: marker created after firing; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: marker should exist after firing\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nCALLS_1=$(wc -l < \"$STUB_CALLS\")\n# 2nd invocation with same SID — marker exists → no-op\nrun_hook \"tc6\"\nCALLS_2=$(wc -l < \"$STUB_CALLS\")\nif [ \"$CALLS_1\" != \"$CALLS_2\" ]; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\n# Stage stub sync-tracker-update.sh that records its arguments\nSYNC_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks\"\nmkdir -p \"$SYNC_DIR\"\nSTUB=\"$SYNC_DIR/sync-tracker-update.sh\"\nSTUB_CALLS=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.stub-calls.log\"\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$STUB_CALLS\")\"\n\nstage_stub() {\n  cat > \"$STUB\" <<'STUBEOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Stub: record args to STUB_CALLS log\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"${STUB_CALLS:-/tmp/stub-calls.log}\")\"\necho \"CALLED: $*\" >> \"${STUB_CALLS:-/tmp/stub-calls.log}\"\nexit 0\nSTUBEOF\n  chmod +x \"$STUB\"\n}\n\nLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n\nrun_hook() {\n  local sid=\"${1:-tc-session}\"\n  STUB_CALLS=\"$STUB_CALLS\" CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" CLAUDE_SESSION_ID=\"$sid\" \\\n    bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\" \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a\"\n  : > \"$STUB_CALLS\"\n}\n\nmarker_path() {\n  echo \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.sync-tracker-fired-$1\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: sync-tracker-update.sh missing → silent no-op ---\nclean_state\nrm -f \"$STUB\"\nrun_hook \"tc1\"\nif [ -f \"$(marker_path tc1)\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: marker should NOT be created when stub missing\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: sync-tracker-update.sh missing → silent no-op\"\n\n# --- TC2: marker already exists → exit 0 idempotent (no fire) ---\nclean_state\nstage_stub\ntouch \"$(marker_path tc2)\"\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\"\necho \"# ADR\" > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions/D-099-test.md\"\nrun_hook \"tc2\"\nif [ -s \"$STUB_CALLS\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: stub should NOT be called when marker exists\"\n  cat \"$STUB_CALLS\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: marker exists → idempotent no-op\"\n\n# --- TC3: new ADR mtime <6h → SCOPE charter_match fired ---\nclean_state\nstage_stub\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\"\necho \"# ADR D-099 test\" > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions/D-099-test.md\"\nrun_hook \"tc3\"\nif [ ! -s \"$STUB_CALLS\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: stub should be called for new ADR\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"SCOPE charter_match\" \"$STUB_CALLS\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected 'SCOPE charter_match' invocation\"\n  cat \"$STUB_CALLS\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: new ADR <6h → SCOPE charter_match fired\"\n\n# --- TC4: HIGH drift today → DECISION_ROUTING drift_signal fired ---\nclean_state\nstage_stub\nTODAY=\"$(date -u +%Y-%m-%d)\"\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.drift-signals.log\" <<EOF\n[${TODAY}T10:00:00+00:00] D1-LOC-CEILING severity=HIGH file=foo.md\n[${TODAY}T10:01:00+00:00] D5-MISSING-CITATION severity=HIGH file=bar.md\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc4\"\nif ! grep -q \"DECISION_ROUTING drift_signal\" \"$STUB_CALLS\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected 'DECISION_ROUTING drift_signal' invocation\"\n  cat \"$STUB_CALLS\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: HIGH drift today → DECISION_ROUTING drift_signal fired\"\n\n# --- TC5: Q&A in answered/ <6h → DECISION_ROUTING q_and_a_resolution ---\nclean_state\nstage_stub\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\"\necho \"# answered bundle\" > \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/2026-05-05-bundle.md\"\nrun_hook \"tc5\"\nif ! grep -q \"DECISION_ROUTING q_and_a_resolution\" \"$STUB_CALLS\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected 'DECISION_ROUTING q_and_a_resolution' invocation\"\n  cat \"$STUB_CALLS\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC5: Q&A answered <6h → q_and_a_resolution fired\"\n\n# --- TC6: marker created after firing; 2nd invocation idempotent ---\nclean_state\nstage_stub\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\"\necho \"# ADR\" > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions/D-100-test.md\"\nrun_hook \"tc6\"\nif [ ! -f \"$(marker_path tc6)\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: marker should exist after firing\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nCALLS_1=$(wc -l < \"$STUB_CALLS\")\n# 2nd invocation with same SID — marker exists → no-op\nrun_hook \"tc6\"\nCALLS_2=$(wc -l < \"$STUB_CALLS\")\nif [ \"$CALLS_1\" != \"$CALLS_2\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: 2nd invocation should NOT call stub again (1=$CALLS_1 2=$CALLS_2)\"\n  cat \"$STUB_CALLS\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC6: marker created + 2nd invocation idempotent\"\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== ALL FIRING-TESTS PASSED (6/6) ===\"\necho \"sync-tracker-auto-update.sh externally-observable behavior verified.\"\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cfcfd28a-6479-4d7f-a1b8-3b43c89fbcb6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"2d4de12e-9e68-4ab3-8df9-8d870ca0e73d","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

grep -q '\"matched_vi\":\"không đúng\"' \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected matched_vi=\\\"không đúng\\\"\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q '\"matched_en\":\"wrong\"' \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected matched_en=\\\"wrong\\\"\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC5: combined VI+EN → both matched fields populated\"","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for correction-rate-tracker.sh (Phase 3.5 T7 retrofit; S62).\n#\n# Hook purpose (D-004 § Tracking Instrumentation Q2=A; UserPromptSubmit):\n# detect VI/EN correction patterns in user prompt, append JSONL line to\n# .correction-rate.log with bucket_50k from current .transcript-tokens.\n# Non-blocking; pure telemetry.\n#\n# Test strategy: stage temp PROJECT_DIR; pipe JSON {prompt: \"...\"} to hook\n# stdin; assert .correction-rate.log JSONL contents + .session-hooks.log entry.\n#\n# 6 test cases:\n#   TC1 — empty stdin → exit 0; no log entry written\n#   TC2 — neutral prompt \"ok continue\" → no match → no JSONL appended\n#   TC3 — VI correction \"không phải\" → JSONL with matched_vi populated\n#   TC4 — EN correction \"no, that's wrong\" → JSONL with matched_en populated\n#   TC5 — both VI + EN match → JSONL with BOTH matched_vi AND matched_en\n#   TC6 — bucket_50k computed from .transcript-tokens (e.g. 75432 → 50000)\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../correction-rate-tracker.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\ncommand -v node >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo \"SKIP: node required (hook uses node -e)\"; exit 0; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.correction-rate.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nTOK_FILE=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.transcript-tokens\"\n\nrun_hook() {\n  local prompt=\"$1\" sid=\"${2:-tc-session}\"\n  local payload\n  payload=$(node -e \"process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({prompt: process.argv[1]}))\" \"$prompt\")\n  printf '%s' \"$payload\" | CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" CLAUDE_SESSION_ID=\"$sid\" \\\n    bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: empty stdin → exit 0; no log written ---\nclean_state\nprintf '' | CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" CLAUDE_SESSION_ID=\"tc1\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\nif [ -f \"$LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: empty stdin should NOT create log file\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: empty stdin → no log written\"\n\n# --- TC2: neutral prompt \"ok continue\" → no match → no JSONL ---\nclean_state\nrun_hook \"ok continue please\" \"tc2\"\nif [ -f \"$LOG\" ] && [ -s \"$LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: neutral prompt should NOT trigger JSONL append\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: neutral prompt → no JSONL appended\"\n\n# --- TC3: VI correction \"không phải\" → JSONL with matched_vi ---\nclean_state\nrun_hook \"không phải, làm lại đi\" \"tc3\"\nif [ ! -f \"$LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: VI correction should write JSONL log file\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q '\"matched_vi\"' \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected matched_vi field in JSONL\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q 'không phải' \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected 'không phải' in matched_vi\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: VI correction → JSONL with matched_vi populated\"\n\n# --- TC4: EN correction \"no, that's wrong\" → JSONL with matched_en ---\nclean_state\nrun_hook \"no, that's wrong, please undo\" \"tc4\"\nif [ ! -f \"$LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: EN correction should write JSONL log file\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q '\"matched_en\"' \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected matched_en field\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n# At least one of the EN keywords should appear in matched_en value\nif ! grep -qE '(wrong|no|undo)' \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected EN keyword in matched_en\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: EN correction → JSONL with matched_en populated\"\n\n# --- TC5: both VI + EN match → JSONL with BOTH ---\nclean_state\nrun_hook \"không đúng — that's wrong\" \"tc5\"\nif [ ! -f \"$LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: combined correction should write JSONL\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n# Both matched_vi and matched_en should be non-null in same line\nif ! awk -F'\"matched_vi\":' 'NR==1 { sub(/^[^,]*,/,\"\",$2); print $2 }' \"$LOG\" | grep -q \"không đúng\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: matched_vi should contain 'không đúng'\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"wrong\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: matched_en should contain 'wrong'\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC5: combined VI+EN → both matched fields populated\"\n\n# --- TC6: bucket_50k computed from .transcript-tokens ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\necho \"75432\" > \"$TOK_FILE\"\nrun_hook \"không phải\" \"tc6\"\nif [ ! -f \"$LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: should write JSONL\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n# 75432 → bucket = floor(75432/50000)*50000 = 50000\nif ! grep -q '\"bucket_50k\":50000' \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: expected bucket_50k=50000 from tokens=75432\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q '\"tokens_at_correction\":75432' \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: expected tokens_at_correction=75432\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC6: bucket_50k computed from .transcript-tokens (75432→50000)\"\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== ALL FIRING-TESTS PASSED (6/6) ===\"\necho \"correction-rate-tracker.sh externally-observable behavior verified.\"\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":110,"oldLines":14,"newStart":110,"newLines":15,"lines":["   echo \"FAIL TC5: combined correction should write JSONL\"","   exit 1"," fi","-# Both matched_vi and matched_en should be non-null in same line","-if ! grep -q '\"matched_en\":\"wrong\"' \"$LOG\"; then","+  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected matched_en=\\\"wrong\\\"\"","   cat \"$LOG\"","   exit 1"," fi"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b6ffaed3-97fa-4755-8f11-a856e035cbfc","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"2d4de12e-9e68-4ab3-8df9-8d870ca0e73d","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Plus DR1/DR3/DR6/DR8\n# domain checks. Append violations to .drift-signals.log; exit 2 in\n# STOCKFORGE_DRIFT_STRICT=1 + HIGH violations.\n#\n# Test strategy: stage temp PROJECT_DIR with various fixture trees;\n# invoke hook; assert .drift-signals.log entries + exit code.\n#\n# 6 test cases:\n#   TC1 — clean state (no agents/skills/commands/data) → no violations\n#   TC2 — agent file 250 LOC > 200 ceiling, overrun ≥ 20% → D1-LOC-CEILING HIGH\n#   TC3 — agent file 220 LOC > 200, overrun < 20% → D1-LOC-CEILING MEDIUM\n#   TC4 — data file with \"18% confidence\" but no source/as_of + no calib → D5/D8 HIGH\n#   TC5 — D9 path leak: skill referencing learning-data/events → D9-LEARNING-PATH-LEAK\n#   TC6 — STOCKFORGE_DRIFT_STRICT=1 + HIGH violation → exit 2 (block)\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../drift-signals-D1-D9.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.drift-signals.log\"\n\nrun_hook() {\n  local strict=\"${1:-0}\"\n  STOCKFORGE_DRIFT_STRICT=\"$strict\" CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" \\\n    bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1\n  echo $?\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/.claude\" \"$TEMPDIR/specs\" \"$TEMPDIR/packages\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\" \"$TEMPDIR/.claude/agents\" \\\n           \"$TEMPDIR/.claude/skills\" \"$TEMPDIR/.claude/commands\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: clean state → no violations ---\nclean_state\nEXIT=$(run_hook 0)\nif [ \"$EXIT\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: clean state should exit 0 (got $EXIT)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$LOG\" ] && [ -s \"$LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: clean state should NOT log violations\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: clean state → no violations logged\"\n\n# --- TC2: agent 250 LOC > 200 ceiling, overrun ≥ 20% → D1 HIGH ---\nclean_state\n# Generate a 250-line agent file\nyes '# line' 2>/dev/null | head -250 > \"$TEMPDIR/.claude/agents/big-agent.md\"\nEXIT=$(run_hook 0)\nif [ \"$EXIT\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: should exit 0 in non-strict mode\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"D1-LOC-CEILING\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected D1-LOC-CEILING entry\"\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"severity=HIGH\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected severity=HIGH (250/200 = 25% overrun ≥ 20%)\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"lines=250\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected lines=250 in detail\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: agent 250 LOC > 200 → D1-LOC-CEILING HIGH\"\n\n# --- TC3: agent 220 LOC, overrun 10% < 20% → D1 MEDIUM ---\nclean_state\nyes '# line' 2>/dev/null | head -220 > \"$TEMPDIR/.claude/agents/midsize-agent.md\"\nEXIT=$(run_hook 0)\nif ! grep -q \"D1-LOC-CEILING\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected D1-LOC-CEILING entry\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"severity=MEDIUM\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected severity=MEDIUM (220/200 = 10% overrun < 20%)\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: agent 220 LOC > 200 (10% overrun) → D1-LOC-CEILING MEDIUM\"\n\n# --- TC4: data file with confidence claim but no calibration → D5+D8 ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log\" \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/calibration\"\n# Note: file mtime must be recent (mmin -1440)\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/calibration/test-thesis.md\" <<'EOF'\n# Thesis claim\nStock returned 18% over past year with high confidence.\nThe ROE is around 25% based on Q2 reports.\nEOF\nEXIT=$(run_hook 0)\nif ! grep -q \"D5-MISSING-CITATION\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected D5-MISSING-CITATION (numeric without source/as_of)\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"D8-CONFIDENCE-NO-CALIB\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected D8-CONFIDENCE-NO-CALIB (high confidence without n_samples/hit_rate)\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: numeric+confidence without calibration → D5+D8 HIGH\"\n\n# --- TC5: D9 path leak — skill referencing learning-data/events ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/.claude/skills/leaky-skill\"\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/.claude/skills/leaky-skill/SKILL.md\" <<'EOF'\n# Leaky Skill\nThis skill reads from learning-data/events directly (anti-pattern).\nEOF\nEXIT=$(run_hook 0)\nif ! grep -q \"D9-LEARNING-PATH-LEAK\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected D9-LEARNING-PATH-LEAK\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"category=read-path\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected category=read-path\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC5: skill refs learning-data/events → D9-LEARNING-PATH-LEAK\"\n\n# --- TC6: STOCKFORGE_DRIFT_STRICT=1 + HIGH → exit 2 ---\nclean_state\nyes '# line' 2>/dev/null | head -250 > \"$TEMPDIR/.claude/agents/big-agent.md\"\nEXIT=$(run_hook 1)\nif [ \"$EXIT\" != \"2\" ]; {"parentUuid":"94ea2e3a-3746-4fa7-9531-b89c23fab3e6","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"4686afe4-d9b1-4f91-af19-102f3150a934","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01RRbzn4MoSMvZRQrgGvSg9R","type":"tool_result","content":"The file C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\metric-failure-mode-rate-fire-test.sh has been updated successfully."}]},"uuid":"36f860a1-0307-4de6-b18b-3b4155d6b405","timestamp":"2026-05-05T16:09:41.124Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\metric-failure-mode-rate-fire-test.sh","oldString":"latest_metric_file() {\n  ls -t \"$INDEX_DIR\"/metrics-*.json 2>/dev/null | head -1\n}","newString":"latest_metric_file() {\n  # `ls + head` with pipefail can SIGPIPE / propagate ls-no-match exit;\n  # `|| true` ensures helper returns clean even when no matches.\n  ls -t \"$INDEX_DIR\"/metrics-*.json 2>/dev/null | head -1 || true\n}","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for metric-failure-mode-rate.sh (Phase 3.5 T7 retrofit; S62).\n#\n# Hook purpose (S12 Karpathy framing; CLI tool — invoked by learning-loop-metric-check):\n# compute count(events.failure_mode != null) / count(events) over\n# learning-data/events/*.ndjson. Output: stdout summary + JSON file at\n# learning-data/index/metrics-<TS>.json.\n# CLI flags: --quiet (suppress stdout), --window N (last N events only).\n#\n# Test strategy: stage temp PROJECT_DIR with various events fixtures;\n# invoke CLI; assert stdout summary + index JSON file content.\n#\n# 6 test cases:\n#   TC1 — events dir absent → total=0 + JSON file written\n#   TC2 — events with no failure_mode → with_fm=0 rate=0\n#   TC3 — events with failure_mode → with_fm>0 + dist populated\n#   TC4 — --window 2 → only last 2 events counted\n#   TC5 — --quiet → no stdout but JSON file still written\n#   TC6 — invalid --window value → graceful exit (trap), no JSON written\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../metric-failure-mode-rate.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\ncommand -v node >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo \"SKIP: node required (hook uses node -e)\"; exit 0; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nEVENTS_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/learning-data/events\"\nINDEX_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/learning-data/index\"\n\nrun_hook() {\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" \"$@\" 2>/dev/null || true\n}\n\nrun_hook_capture_stdout() {\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" \"$@\" 2>/dev/null || true\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$INDEX_DIR\"\n}\n\nlatest_metric_file() {\n  ls -t \"$INDEX_DIR\"/metrics-*.json 2>/dev/null | head -1\n}\n\n# --- TC1: events dir absent → total=0 ---\nclean_state\nrun_hook --quiet >/dev/null\nJSON_FILE=$(latest_metric_file)\nif [ -z \"$JSON_FILE\" ] || [ ! -f \"$JSON_FILE\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: index JSON file should be written even when events absent\"\n  ls -la \"$INDEX_DIR\" 2>/dev/null\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q '\"total\":0' \"$JSON_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected total=0\"\n  cat \"$JSON_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: events dir absent → total=0 + JSON written\"\n\n# --- TC2: events with no failure_mode → with_fm=0 ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$EVENTS_DIR\"\ncat > \"$EVENTS_DIR/2026-05-05.ndjson\" <<'EOF'\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"foo\"}}\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:01:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"bar\"}}\nEOF\nrun_hook --quiet >/dev/null\nJSON_FILE=$(latest_metric_file)\nif ! grep -q '\"total\":2' \"$JSON_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected total=2\"\n  cat \"$JSON_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q '\"with_fm\":0' \"$JSON_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected with_fm=0\"\n  cat \"$JSON_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q '\"rate\":0' \"$JSON_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected rate=0\"\n  cat \"$JSON_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: no failure_mode in events → rate=0\"\n\n# --- TC3: events with failure_mode → with_fm>0 + dist ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$EVENTS_DIR\"\ncat > \"$EVENTS_DIR/2026-05-05.ndjson\" <<'EOF'\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"foo\",\"failure_mode\":\"timeout\"}}\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:01:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"bar\"}}\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:02:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"baz\",\"failure_mode\":\"validation\"}}\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:03:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"qux\",\"failure_mode\":\"timeout\"}}\nEOF\nrun_hook --quiet >/dev/null\nJSON_FILE=$(latest_metric_file)\nif ! grep -q '\"total\":4' \"$JSON_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected total=4\"\n  cat \"$JSON_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q '\"with_fm\":3' \"$JSON_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected with_fm=3\"\n  cat \"$JSON_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q '\"timeout\":2' \"$JSON_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected dist timeout=2\"\n  cat \"$JSON_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q '\"validation\":1' \"$JSON_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected dist validation=1\"\n  cat \"$JSON_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: events with failure_mode → with_fm=3, dist populated\"\n\n# --- TC4: --window 2 → only last 2 events counted ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$EVENTS_DIR\"\ncat > \"$EVENTS_DIR/2026-05-05.ndjson\" <<'EOF'\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"e1\",\"failure_mode\":\"timeout\"}}\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:01:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"e2\",\"failure_mode\":\"timeout\"}}\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:02:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"e3\"}}\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:03:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"e4\",\"failure_mode\":\"validation\"}}\nEOF\nrun_hook --quiet --window 2 >/dev/null\nJSON_FILE=$(latest_metric_file)\nif ! grep -q '\"total\":2' \"$JSON_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected total=2 (window of 2 events)\"\n  cat \"$JSON_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q '\"mode\":\"windowed:2\"' \"$JSON_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected mode=windowed:2\"\n  cat \"$JSON_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n# Window covers e3 (no fm) + e4 (validation) → with_fm=1\nif ! grep -q '\"with_fm\":1' \"$JSON_FILE\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected with_fm=1 (last 2 events: e3 no-fm, e4 validation)\"\n  cat \"$JSON_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: --window 2 → only last 2 events counted\"\n\n# --- TC5: --quiet → no stdout but JSON still written ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$EVENTS_DIR\"\ncat > \"$EVENTS_DIR/2026-05-05.ndjson\" <<'EOF'\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"foo\"}}\nEOF\nSTDOUT=$(run_hook_capture_stdout --quiet)\nif [ -n \"$STDOUT\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: --quiet should suppress stdout (got: $STDOUT)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nJSON_FILE=$(latest_metric_file)\nif [ -z \"$JSON_FILE\" ] || [ ! -f \"$JSON_FILE\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: --quiet should still write JSON file\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC5: --quiet → no stdout + JSON file written\"\n\n# --- TC6: invalid --window value → graceful exit (no JSON) ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$EVENTS_DIR\"\ncat > \"$EVENTS_DIR/2026-05-05.ndjson\" <<'EOF'\n{\"ts\":\"2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\",\"payload\":{\"event\":\"foo\"}}\nEOF\n# --window without numeric arg → triggers stderr error; trap exits 0\nrun_hook --window not-a-number >/dev/null 2>&1\nJSON_FILE=$(latest_metric_file)\nif [ -n \"$JSON_FILE\" ] && [ -f \"$JSON_FILE\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: invalid --window should NOT produce JSON file\"\n  cat \"$JSON_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC6: invalid --window value → graceful exit (no JSON)\"\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== ALL FIRING-TESTS PASSED (6/6) ===\"\necho \"metric-failure-mode-rate.sh externally-observable behavior verified.\"\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":46,"oldLines":7,"newStart":46,"newLines":9,"lines":[" }"," "," latest_metric_file() {","-  ls -t \"$INDEX_DIR\"/metrics-*.json 2>/dev/null | head -1","+  # `ls + head` with pipefail can SIGPIPE / propagate ls-no-match exit;","+  # `|| true` ensures helper returns clean even when no matches.","+  ls -t \"$INDEX_DIR\"/metrics-*.json 2>/dev/null | head -1 || true"," }"," "," # --- TC1: events dir absent → total=0 ---"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"94ea2e3a-3746-4fa7-9531-b89c23fab3e6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"2d4de12e-9e68-4ab3-8df9-8d870ca0e73d","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Mix of `if grep -qE` exempt + `\\|\\| true` / `\\|\\| echo 0` alt-guards. | **NO** |\n| drift-rollup-daily | 107 | ~7 | Class A `^[$TODAY` + `^[` anchored timestamp prefixes + Class C `\\|\\| true` guards on `grep -c severity=*` and `grep -oE 'file=...'`. | **NO** |\n| metric-failure-mode-rate | 103 | 0 | Pure node.js + find/sort/xargs. Doctrine cleanly applied.\n\n**Notable observation**: 2 of 6 hooks (correction-rate-aggregator, metric-failure-mode-rate) use 0 greps and rely entirely on `node -e` for JSON aggregation. This is doctrine-aligned — bash-hook-lint Check 7/8 naturally doesn't apply to node-based hooks. Same pattern as S60 (checkpoint-write-end-turn-watchdog) + S61 (dispatch-jsonl-recorder + loc-ceiling-check). Across S60+S61+S62, **5 of 16 retrofit hooks are 0-grep node-based** (~31%) — doctrine cleanly handles both bash+grep and node-based hooks.\n\n---\n\n## 6 NEW companion firing-tests (36 NEW TCs)\n\nPer L-S51-1 + L-S59-1 doctrine: target externally observable behavior (log writes / marker files / stdout JSON / file existence / exit codes), NOT internal implementation.\n\n### #1 — correction-rate-tracker-fire-test.sh (UserPromptSubmit; 6 TCs)\n- TC1: empty stdin → no log written\n- TC2: neutral prompt \"ok continue\" → no JSONL appended\n- TC3: VI correction \"không phải\" → JSONL with matched_vi populated\n- TC4: EN correction \"no, that's wrong\" → JSONL with matched_en\n- TC5: combined VI+EN correction → both matched fields populated\n- TC6: bucket_50k computed from .transcript-tokens (75432 → 50000)\n\n### #2 — correction-rate-aggregator-fire-test.sh (Stop; 6 TCs)\n- TC1: log file missing → \"no log entries; skip\" + no digest\n- TC2: log file empty → \"no log entries; skip\" + no digest\n- TC3: 1 entry → this_session_corrections=1\n- TC4: multi-bucket → bucket counts populated correctly (0:1, 50000:2)\n- TC5: 3 distinct sessions → all_sessions_distinct_count=3\n- TC6: malformed JSONL line → skip silently; valid lines aggregated (2/3)\n\n### #3 — drift-signals-D1-D9-fire-test.sh (Stop; 6 TCs)\n- TC1: clean state → no violations logged\n- TC2: agent 250 LOC > 200 ceiling, overrun ≥20% → D1-LOC-CEILING HIGH\n- TC3: agent 220 LOC > 200, overrun 10% < 20% → D1-LOC-CEILING MEDIUM\n- TC4: data file with confidence + numeric without source/as_of/calib → D5+D8 HIGH\n- TC5: skill referencing learning-data/events → D9-LEARNING-PATH-LEAK\n- TC6: STOCKFORGE_DRIFT_STRICT=1 + HIGH violation → exit 2 (block)\n\n### #4 — drift-rollup-daily-fire-test.sh (Stop; 6 TCs)\n- TC1: raw log absent → \"raw log absent; skip\" + no rollup\n- TC2: raw log → rollup written with summary section\n- TC3: rollup current (raw mtime ≤ rollup) → \"rollup current; skip\"; rollup unchanged\n- TC4: severity counts populated correctly (HIGH:2, MEDIUM:1, LOW:1)\n- TC5: distinct files populated in 'Files referenced' section (alpha+beta)\n- TC6: HIGH entries → 'Last 10 HIGH-severity entries' code block populated\n\n### #5 — metric-failure-mode-rate-fire-test.sh (CLI; 6 TCs, with `command -v node` skip-guard)\n- TC1: events dir absent → total=0 + JSON file written\n- TC2: events with no failure_mode → with_fm=0, rate=0\n- TC3: events with failure_mode → with_fm=3, dist populated (timeout:2, validation:1)\n- TC4: --window 2 → only last 2 events counted (mode=windowed:2)\n- TC5: --quiet → no stdout but JSON file written\n- TC6: invalid --window value → graceful exit (no JSON file)\n\n### #6 — sync-tracker-auto-update-fire-test.sh (Stop; 6 TCs)\n- TC1: sync-tracker-update.sh missing → silent no-op (no marker)\n- TC2: marker exists → idempotent no-op (stub not called)\n- TC3: new ADR <6h → SCOPE charter_match fired\n- TC4: HIGH drift today → DECISION_ROUTING drift_signal fired\n- TC5: Q&A in answered/ <6h → DECISION_ROUTING q_and_a_resolution fired\n- TC6: marker created + 2nd invocation idempotent\n\n**Result**: 36/36 PASS after fix iteration.\n\n---\n\n## Pre-deploy iteration-bugs caught at firing-test stage (per L-S52-3 SUCCESS path)\n\n3 iteration-bugs caught + fixed in same session BEFORE deployment — this is the L-S51-1 SUCCESS path operating correctly. **Not catalogued as M-S62-X mistakes** (mistakes are bugs that REACH deployment per L-S52-3 doctrine).\n\n1. **correction-rate-tracker TC5 assertion regex over-engineered**: original `awk -F'\"matched_vi\":' 'NR==1 { sub(/^[^,]*,/,\"\",$2); print $2 }' \\| grep -q \"không đúng\"` was brittle (depending on JSON field order). Fixed by direct substring `grep -q '\"matched_vi\":\"không đúng\"'`. Lesson reinforces L-S59-2 (two-stage / direct grep beats regex chaining for multi-substring assertions).\n\n2. **drift-signals-D1-D9 TC2/TC3 SIGPIPE**: `yes '# line' \\| head -250` triggered SIGPIPE (exit 141) under `set -o pipefail` — `yes` continues writing after `head` exits, gets EPIPE, pipefail propagates. Fixed by replacing with `seq 1 250 \\| sed 's/.*/# line/'` (deterministic line count, no SIGPIPE risk). **NEW LESSON candidate** (L-S62-1 below).\n\n3. **metric-failure-mode-rate TC6 ls-no-match propagation**: helper `latest_metric_file() { ls -t \"$INDEX_DIR\"/metrics-*.json 2>/dev/null \\| head -1 }` returned non-zero when no JSONs match → pipefail+errexit propagated → script aborted before assertion. Fixed by appending `\\|\\| true` to helper. Each rule earned through real experience (post-mortem, drift detection, user correction).\n4\t> Rules here have higher status than convention but lower than constitution.\n5\t\n6\t## Rule Format\n7\t\n8\tUse this template:\n9\t\n10\t```markdown\n11\t### YYYY-MM-DD: [Short rule name]\n12\t**Context**: What situation triggered learning this\n13\t**Rule**: The actionable rule\n14\t**Anti-example**: What was done wrong\n15\t**Correct example**: What should be done instead\n16\t**Severity**: critical | high | medium | low\n17\t**Auto-detect**: yes/no — can a drift signal catch this?\n18\t```\n19\t\n20\t---\n21\t\n22\t## Initial Rules (from charter design)\n23\t\n24\t### 2026-04-23: LLM Never Outputs Numbers\n25\t**Context**: Stock domain risk — LLM-generated numbers in finance lead to real money loss\n26\t**Rule**: LLM never returns numerical values as natural-language output. All numbers come from deterministic Python functions invoked via tool use. LLM only interprets results.\n27\t**Anti-example**:\n28\t```\n29\tLLM output: \"Based on my analysis, the company's ROE is approximately 18%, P/E is around 12, and intrinsic value is roughly 95,000 VND per share.\"\n30\t```\n31\t**Correct example**:\n32\t```\n33\tLLM tool calls:\n34\t  compute_roe(net_income=2_345B, equity=13_120B) → 17.87%\n35\t  compute_pe(price=85_000, eps=7_054) → 12.05\n36\t  compute_intrinsic_value_dcf(...) → 94_523 VND\n37\tLLM output: \"ROE = 17.87% (FY2024), P/E = 12.05 (TTM), DCF intrinsic value = 94,523 VND. Versus current price 85,000 VND, suggests ~11% margin of safety.\"\n38\t```\n39\t**Severity**: critical\n40\t**Auto-detect**: yes — regex for \"approximately\", \"around\", \"roughly\" + numbers in LLM output\n41\t\n42\t### 2026-04-23: Bear Case Required for Every Thesis\n43\t**Context**: Confirmation bias is the #1 retail investor mistake\n44\t**Rule**: Every thesis output must include substantive bear case (≥3 distinct points, each grounded in evidence). System refuses to render thesis without it.\n45\t**Anti-example**: Thesis with bull case + risks section that says \"market volatility, regulatory changes\" (boilerplate)\n46\t**Correct example**: Bear case with specific points like \"Q3 inventory turnover dropped 23% YoY suggesting demand weakness\", \"Major competitor X launched directly competitive product Y in March 2026\", \"Net working capital deterioration of 15% indicates cash flow strain\"\n47\t**Severity**: high\n48\t**Auto-detect**: yes — bear case length + specificity check\n49\t\n50\t### 2026-04-23: Confidence Requires Calibration Data"}]},"uuid":"eb88ad9d-0be9-4973-94e6-6aed1a743691","timestamp":"2026-05-05T16:17:55.304Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\memory\\agent-notes.md","content":"# Agent Notes — Learned Rules\n\n> Append-mostly. Each rule earned through real experience (post-mortem, drift detection, user correction).\n> Rules here have higher status than convention but lower than constitution.\n\n## Rule Format\n\nUse this template:\n\n```markdown\n### YYYY-MM-DD: [Short rule name]\n**Context**: What situation triggered learning this\n**Rule**: The actionable rule\n**Anti-example**: What was done wrong\n**Correct example**: What should be done instead\n**Severity**: critical | high | medium | low\n**Auto-detect**: yes/no — can a drift signal catch this?\n```\n\n---\n\n## Initial Rules (from charter design)\n\n### 2026-04-23: LLM Never Outputs Numbers\n**Context**: Stock domain risk — LLM-generated numbers in finance lead to real money loss\n**Rule**: LLM never returns numerical values as natural-language output. All numbers come from deterministic Python functions invoked via tool use. LLM only interprets results.\n**Anti-example**:\n```\nLLM output: \"Based on my analysis, the company's ROE is approximately 18%, P/E is around 12, and intrinsic value is roughly 95,000 VND per share.\"\n```\n**Correct example**:\n```\nLLM tool calls:\n  compute_roe(net_income=2_345B, equity=13_120B) → 17.87%\n  compute_pe(price=85_000, eps=7_054) → 12.05\n  compute_intrinsic_value_dcf(...) → 94_523 VND\nLLM output: \"ROE = 17.87% (FY2024), P/E = 12.05 (TTM), DCF intrinsic value = 94,523 VND. Versus current price 85,000 VND, suggests ~11% margin of safety.\"\n```\n**Severity**: critical\n**Auto-detect**: yes — regex for \"approximately\", \"around\", \"roughly\" + numbers in LLM output\n\n### 2026-04-23: Bear Case Required for Every Thesis\n**Context**: Confirmation bias is the #1 retail investor mistake\n**Rule**: Every thesis output must include substantive bear case (≥3 distinct points, each grounded in evidence). System refuses to render thesis without it.\n**Anti-example**: Thesis with bull case + risks section that says \"market volatility, regulatory changes\" (boilerplate)\n**Correct example**: Bear case with specific points like \"Q3 inventory turnover dropped 23% YoY suggesting demand weakness\", \"Major competitor X launched directly competitive product Y in March 2026\", \"Net working capital deterioration of 15% indicates cash flow strain\"\n**Severity**: high\n**Auto-detect**: yes — bear case length + specificity check\n\n### 2026-04-23: Confidence Requires Calibration Data","numLines":50,"startLine":1,"totalLines":1338}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"27750418-e8a4-486d-b62d-48c163a0523f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"2d4de12e-9e68-4ab3-8df9-8d870ca0e73d","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"da02b5a8-d210-4d79-9332-45f4e786b212","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"4686afe4-d9b1-4f91-af19-102f3150a934","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Eg4ijBv2XCDncwf3BSL5gv","type":"tool_result","content":"- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S59-T7-retrofit-batch-5-hooks.log` § Pre-deploy iteration-bug catches","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"70214280-5cf0-49c1-aa5a-032f780d2610","timestamp":"2026-05-05T16:18:23.167Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S59-T7-retrofit-batch-5-hooks.log` § Pre-deploy iteration-bug catches","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"da02b5a8-d210-4d79-9332-45f4e786b212","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"2d4de12e-9e68-4ab3-8df9-8d870ca0e73d","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"70214280-5cf0-49c1-aa5a-032f780d2610","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01Ku1QmGyJk9V1V43RzzTWsy","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_019c6AcMvDaDnboWNWHTUcjw","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\memory\\agent-notes.md","old_string":"- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S59-T7-retrofit-batch-5-hooks.log` § Pre-deploy iteration-bug catches","new_string":"- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S59-T7-retrofit-batch-5-hooks.log` § Pre-deploy iteration-bug catches\n\n### L-S62-1 — Firing-test fixtures must avoid `yes | head -N` under `set -o pipefail` (SIGPIPE risk)\n**Date**: 2026-05-05\n**Context**: S62 firing-test for drift-signals-D1-D9 hit exit code 141 (SIGPIPE) after TC1 PASSed, aborting the test mid-batch. Root cause: TC2 fixture used `yes '# line' | head -250 > file.md` to generate a 250-line agent file. Under `set -euo pipefail`, when `head -250` exits, `yes` keeps writing, gets `EPIPE`, returns non-zero — pipefail propagates the failure → errexit triggers → script aborts before reaching TC3+.\n\n**Rule**: Firing-test fixture generation **must avoid throw-away producer commands piped to `head`/`tail`** under `set -o pipefail`. Use deterministic line-count generators instead:\n```bash\n# AVOID (SIGPIPE risk under pipefail):\nyes '# line' | head -250 > file.md\ncat huge_source | head -100 > truncated.md\n\n# PREFER:\nseq 1 250 | sed 's/.*/# line/' > file.md\nprintf '# line\\n%.0s' {1..250} > file.md   # bash-only\nfor i in $(seq 1 250); do echo '# line'; done > file.md\n```\n\n**Why**:\n- `yes` and `cat huge_file` are **infinite/large producers**: they always have more bytes to write than the consumer (`head`) reads.\n- When `head -N` exits after reading N lines, the OS sends `SIGPIPE` to the producer on its next write.\n- Default behavior: producer terminates with exit 141 (128+13).\n- Under `set -o pipefail`: rightmost non-zero exit propagates → `set -e` triggers → script aborts.\n- Symptom in firing-tests: TC1 (which doesn't use the pattern) PASSes; TC2 (first use of pattern) gets exit 141 mid-script; remaining TCs never run; cumulative regression batch may report fewer hooks than expected.\n\n**How to apply**:\n- For fixed line count: `seq 1 N | sed 's/.*/PATTERN/'` (POSIX, deterministic, exits cleanly).\n- For repeated string: `printf 'PATTERN\\n%.0s' {1..N}` (bash brace expansion).\n- For complex multi-line content: use heredocs (`cat <<EOF`); not piped.\n- Any \"throw-away source\" piped to `head`/`tail` is suspect — review for SIGPIPE risk.\n\n**Validation**: S62 drift-signals-D1-D9-fire-test.sh TC2/TC3 — original `yes '# line' | head -250` reproduced exit 141 deterministically; replaced with `seq 1 250 | sed 's/.*/# line/'`; all 6 TCs PASS post-fix. Caught at firing-test stage = SUCCESS path of L-S51-1 per L-S52-3 doctrine; NOT catalogued as M-S62-X.\n\n**Severity**: low — narrowly applicable to firing-test fixture authoring. But fixture-authoring is the most common pattern in T7 retrofit work, and the failure mode (mid-batch abort) makes diagnosis harder than a clean assertion FAIL.\n\n**Auto-detect**: candidate — bash-hook-lint Check N could grep firing-tests for `yes .* | head\\|cat .* | head -[0-9]`. Defer until ≥2 more occurrences across firing-tests (1-occurrence lesson is borderline; broader sample needed before adding lint check).\n\n**Where tracked**:\n- This entry (L-S62-1)\n- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/drift-signals-D1-D9-fire-test.sh` (post-fix using `seq | sed`)\n- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S62-T7-retrofit-batch-6-hooks-final.log` § Pre-deploy iteration-bugs caught at firing-test stage"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":243,"cache_read_input_tokens":165767,"output_tokens":1678,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":243,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":1678,"cache_read_input_tokens":165767,"cache_creation_input_tokens":243,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":243},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cajj2dtdG7Dauw7QvXtoE","type":"assistant","uuid":"913001d9-461b-4715-8fe7-5298339c3da7","timestamp":"2026-05-05T16:18:49.097Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"2d4de12e-9e68-4ab3-8df9-8d870ca0e73d","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Use deterministic line-count generators instead:","+```bash","+# AVOID (SIGPIPE risk under pipefail):","+yes '# line' | head -250 > file.md","+cat huge_source | head -100 > truncated.md","+","+# PREFER:","+seq 1 250 | sed 's/.*/# line/' > file.md","+printf '# line\\n%.0s' {1..250} > file.md   # bash-only","+for i in $(seq 1 250); do echo '# line'; done > file.md","+```","+","+**Why**:","+- `yes` and `cat huge_file` are **infinite/large producers**: they always have more bytes to write than the consumer (`head`) reads.","+- When `head -N` exits after reading N lines, the OS sends `SIGPIPE` to the producer on its next write.","+- Default behavior: producer terminates with exit 141 (128+13).","+- Under `set -o pipefail`: rightmost non-zero exit propagates → `set -e` triggers → script aborts.","+- Symptom in firing-tests: TC1 (which doesn't use the pattern) PASSes; TC2 (first use of pattern) gets exit 141 mid-script; remaining TCs never run; cumulative regression batch may report fewer hooks than expected.","+","+**How to apply**:","+- For fixed line count: `seq 1 N | sed 's/.*/PATTERN/'` (POSIX, deterministic, exits cleanly).","+- For repeated string: `printf 'PATTERN\\n%.0s' {1..N}` (bash brace expansion).","+- For complex multi-line content: use heredocs (`cat <<EOF`); not piped.","+- Any \"throw-away source\" piped to `head`/`tail` is suspect — review for SIGPIPE risk.","+","+**Validation**: S62 drift-signals-D1-D9-fire-test.sh TC2/TC3 — original `yes '# line' | head -250` reproduced exit 141 deterministically; replaced with `seq 1 250 | sed 's/.*/# line/'`; all 6 TCs PASS post-fix. Caught at firing-test stage = SUCCESS path of L-S51-1 per L-S52-3 doctrine; NOT catalogued as M-S62-X.","+","+**Severity**: low — narrowly applicable to firing-test fixture authoring. But fixture-authoring is the most common pattern in T7 retrofit work, and the failure mode (mid-batch abort) makes diagnosis harder than a clean assertion FAIL.","+","+**Auto-detect**: candidate — bash-hook-lint Check N could grep firing-tests for `yes .* | head\\|cat .* | head -[0-9]`. Defer until ≥2 more occurrences across firing-tests (1-occurrence lesson is borderline; broader sample needed before adding lint check).","+","+**Where tracked**:","+- This entry (L-S62-1)","+- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/drift-signals-D1-D9-fire-test.sh` (post-fix using `seq | sed`)","+- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S62-T7-retrofit-batch-6-hooks-final.log` § Pre-deploy iteration-bugs caught at firing-test stage"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"913001d9-461b-4715-8fe7-5298339c3da7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"2d4de12e-9e68-4ab3-8df9-8d870ca0e73d","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}
